Hover cache-warm — orthogonal to both, and NOT the same mechanism as the existing app-wide JS-chunk prefetch.** Do not confuse this with `resources/js/components/alpine/module-runtime.js`'s `initModulePrefetch()`/`prefetchModule()` (§16 of `resources/js/scriptPattern.md`) — that one prefetches a whole **route's JS bundle** on hovering a left-nav/menu link between different top-level modules (`/projects`, `/energy`, …), solving "the script isn't downloaded yet." This one operates entirely within a single already-loaded page, on the tab buttons themselves, and pre-warms the **server-side query cache** for a technique-1 tab before the user actually clicks it, solving "the DB query hasn't run yet." Wiring, exactly as shipped on `Project` (`app/Livewire/Dashboard/Project/Main.php::warm()`, `resources/js/components/alpine/data/project.js`'s `warmTab()`/`cancelWarm()`, fired by `x-ui.buttons.tab-selector`'s existing shared `x-on:pointerenter="warmTab(id)"`/`x-on:pointerleave="cancelWarm()"`):
```php
#[Renderless]
public function warm(string $tab): void
{
    if (!$this->scopeId || !in_array($tab, self::WARMABLE_TABS, true)) return;
    match ($tab) {
        '<tab>' => app(SomeService::class)->someCachedMethod($this->scopeId), // same method, same cache key the real sibling's #[Computed] already calls
        default => null,
    };
}
```
```js
warmTab(tab) {
    if (!WARMABLE_TABS.includes(tab) || tab === this.$wire.activeTab) return;
    this.cancelWarm();
    this._warmTimer = setTimeout(() => { this._warmTimer = null; this.$wire.warm(tab).catch(() => {}); }, 200);
},
cancelWarm() { if (this._warmTimer) { clearTimeout(this._warmTimer); this._warmTimer = null; } },
```
`warm()` must only ever call into an existing `Cache::`-wrapped service method the real tab's own `#[Computed]` already uses — never a parallel/divergent query path, and never something not already cache-wrapped (warming an uncached query just runs it twice for no benefit). `Project` had an earlier, simpler version of this exact mechanism (a bare `match` that just touched `#[Computed]` properties directly, relying on whichever ones happened to have caching underneath) that was removed as dead code once the module went fully always-mounted (Phase 3) — this version is a deliberate improvement, explicitly targeting named cache-layer calls rather than hoping the touched property happened to be cached.

**Not yet extended to:** `Ths`'s `log` tab (fixed instead with a one-line `@if($activeTab === 'log')` Blade gate, since its eager cost was a plain `@include`, not an independently-mounted sibling component — no defer/remount/warm machinery applies there) and `Profile`'s remaining tabs (already used technique 1's `dynamic-component` shape from the start, just with plain `lazy: true`/`x-intersect` rather than `lazy: 'on-load'`/`x-init` — upgraded to the latter 2026-08-28 for determinism, no warm mechanism added since Profile's tabs are form-heavy with no meaningfully expensive cached read to pre-warm).

## TaskBoard reply notification + three-surface naming + reply-thread restyle

The `TaskNudge` collaborator-recipient loop (notify all active collaborators on reply, not just @mentioned), the three-surface naming (Task's "گفتگوی وظیفه" vs Project's "فعالیت‌ها و نظرات" / "چت زندهٔ تیم"), and the reply-thread restyle to Activity's timeline shape are documented in the module pattern doc: **TaskBoard module → see `app/Livewire/Dashboard/TaskBoard/taskboardPattern.md`** (§7 `reply-row.blade.php`, §11 `TaskNudge`).

## Project settings + approval lifecycle + universal meta tab (v2, cross-reference)

`TaskApprovalNudge` (bell, escalating wording past 24h/48h pending), `TasksPendingApproval` (dot badge), `ManagesTaskModal::approveTask()` (shared trait method both boards call into `ApproveTaskAction`), and the universal 5th `meta` form tab (schema mode vs free mode, same partial/tab-bar on both boards via the shared `ManagesTaskModal`/`TaskBoardPresenter`) are documented in full in the module pattern doc: **TaskBoard module → see `app/Livewire/Dashboard/TaskBoard/taskboardPattern.md`** §19 (19.2 approval, 19.3 meta). Note: `Project\Kanban` does **not** listen for a `project-approve-task` browser/Livewire event — it exposes its own `approveTask()` method directly (same pattern as `cyclePriority`), which `wire:click` on the shared `card.blade.php` calls straight into; don't go looking for an event listener that doesn't exist.

## Tasksheet — single-component read-only mode + event-sourced cross-module report (cross-reference)

The personal performance report module (`App\Livewire\Dashboard\Tasksheet\Main`), its `readOnly`-flag
single-component design for self-view vs. signed-share view, and the event-sourced (not
current-status) completion model are documented in the module pattern doc: **Tasksheet module → see
`app/Livewire/Dashboard/Tasksheet/tasksheetPattern.md`**.
